(3) SBW Berlin: no fixed deadline (rolling, year-round) — the "31 December 2026" dates on some aggregators are not official; €480/month + furnished shared flat + tuition support, up to 48 months, ~20 places; age 18–30, non-profit/social-sector experience required, plus a social project (2–8 hours/week) and a repayment plan if studies or the project stop; health insurance is NOT included. (4) Macquarie: the main international round closed 31 July 2026; the open round is the International Road to Research Scholarship, closing 31 October 2026 at 11pm AEDT, AUD $40,900 for one year (not the $39,700 quoted by aggregators), onshore-in-Australia only, MRes commencing January 2027; "fully funded" overstates a one-year award. (5) Abertay: "fully funded" is false — the International Scholarship is a partial tuition discount of £3,000/year for up to four years (£7,000/year combined with the Principal's Excellence Scholarship), assessed automatically on the application for study with no separate deadline; Bright's intake deadlines (15 Dec / 30 Apr / 31 Aug) are unconfirmed; the Dean's Scholarship (£5,000/year) is aggregator-only.
